# Shared primitive artwork

The glyphs the shared library's neutral components draw with: one monochrome 24×24 SVG per artwork slot, in `currentColor`, with its upstream source. The renderer reads them from this file. Nothing here is pasted into a diagram, and the headings are artwork slots, not component ids: to place a component, use its `infragram:` id or its class from [shared-components.md](shared-components.md).

Generated from the icon sources the repository keeps; do not edit by hand. Generic glyphs are stroked at 1.5 px; brand silhouettes are filled.

## DevOps

### git-branch
